import { Badge, Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { EyeIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useIncidents } from "../hooks/useIncidents";
import { showIncidentNotification } from "../hooks/useNotifications";
import type { IncidentRow } from "../lib/api";
import { STATUS_TEXT } from "../lib/incident";

/**
 * The workspace's incidents (D16, FR-50): polled every 10 s while the tab is visible. Unread ones get a dot
 * and the word "New"; watch-opened ones say "Watch". A row that turns unread raises a browser notification
 * (if allowed), unless it's the incident already open in a visible tab.
 */
export function IncidentSidebar() {
  const [location, navigate] = useLocation();

  const onNewUnread = useCallback(
    (rows: IncidentRow[]) => {
      for (const r of rows) {
        if (location === `/i/${r.id}` && document.visibilityState === "visible") continue;
        showIncidentNotification(r, (id) => navigate(`/i/${id}`));
      }
    },
    [location, navigate],
  );
  const rows = useIncidents(location, onNewUnread);

  if (rows === null) return null;
  if (rows.length === 0) return <p className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">No incidents yet.</p>;
  const unread = rows.filter((r) => r.unread).length;

  return (
    <Sidebar.Group>
      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          Incidents
          {unread > 0 && <Badge variant="secondary">{unread} new</Badge>}
        </span>
      </Sidebar.GroupLabel>
      <Sidebar.Menu>
        {rows.map((r) => {
          const host = safeHost(r.target);
          return (
            <Sidebar.MenuItem key={r.id}>
              <Sidebar.MenuButton href={`/i/${r.id}`} active={location === `/i/${r.id}`} className="h-auto py-1.5">
                <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                  <span className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate font-mono text-xs ${r.unread ? "font-semibold" : ""}`}>
                    {r.unread && <span aria-hidden className="size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-(--cf-fg)" />}
                    <span className="truncate">{host}</span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
                    {r.unread && <span className="font-medium text-(--cf-fg)">New ·</span>}
                    {STATUS_TEXT[r.status]}
                    {r.origin === "watch" && (
                      <>
                        {" · "}
                        <EyeIcon aria-hidden size={12} /> Watch
                      </>
                    )}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </Sidebar.MenuButton>
            </Sidebar.MenuItem>
          );
        })}
      </Sidebar.Menu>
    </Sidebar.Group>
  );
}

function safeHost(target: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(target);
    return u.pathname.length > 1 ? `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}` : u.hostname;
  } catch {
    return target;
  }
}
import { Button, SensitiveInput, Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { BellIcon, BellSlashIcon, CheckCircleIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useNotificationPermission } from "../hooks/useNotifications";
import { getWebhook, removeWebhook, saveWebhook, testWebhook, WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT, type WebhookState } from "../lib/api";

const KIND_TEXT = { slack: "Slack", discord: "Discord" } as const;

/**
 * Notifications (FR-51 to FR-53, TDD §12.1; the TDD's WebhookSettings): browser notifications, switched on from
 * a click, and one Slack or Discord webhook per workspace. Webhooks fire for watched incidents only (D18).
 */
export function NotificationSettings() {
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useNotificationPermission();
  const [hook, setHook] = useState<WebhookState | null>(null);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<{ text: string; alert: boolean } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    void getWebhook().then((r) => live && r.ok && setHook(r.value));
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const run = async <T,>(fn: () => Promise<{ ok: true; value: T } | { ok: false; error: keyof typeof WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT; message?: string }>, ok: (v: T) => void) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setNote(null);
    const r = await fn();
    setBusy(false);
    if (r.ok) ok(r.value);
    else setNote({ text: r.message ?? WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT[r.error], alert: true });
  };

  const save = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!url.trim()) return;
    void run(
      () => saveWebhook(url.trim()),
      (info) => {
        setUrl("");
        setHook({ available: true, webhook: info });
        setNote({ text: "Saved. Watched incidents will post here.", alert: false });
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <Sidebar.Group>
      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>Notifications</Sidebar.GroupLabel>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 px-2 pb-3 break-words whitespace-normal">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Browser</span>
          {permission === "granted" && (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
              <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> On for this browser
            </span>
          )}
          {permission === "default" && (
            <Button size="sm" icon={BellIcon} className="w-fit" onClick={() => void requestPermission()}>
              Turn on notifications
            </Button>
          )}
          {permission === "denied" && (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
              <BellSlashIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> Blocked in your browser settings
            </span>
          )}
          {permission === "unsupported" && <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Not supported in this browser</span>}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Slack or Discord</span>
          <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Posts when a watched site breaks, recovers and is resolved.</span>
          {hook && !hook.available && <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT.webhooks_unavailable}</span>}
          {hook?.available && !hook.webhook && (
            <form onSubmit={save} className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <SensitiveInput label="Webhook URL" size="sm" value={url} onValueChange={setUrl} placeholder="https://discord.com/api/webhooks/…" />
              <Button type="submit" size="sm" className="w-fit" loading={busy} disabled={!url.trim()}>
                Save webhook
              </Button>
            </form>
          )}
          {hook?.webhook && (
            <>
              <span className="text-xs">
                {KIND_TEXT[hook.webhook.kind]} · <span className="font-mono">{hook.webhook.hint}</span>
              </span>
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                <Button size="xs" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(testWebhook, () => setNote({ text: "Test message sent.", alert: false }))}>
                  Send test
                </Button>
                <Button
                  size="xs"
                  variant="secondary-destructive"
                  disabled={busy}
                  onClick={() => void run(removeWebhook, () => setHook({ available: true, webhook: null }))}
                >
                  Remove
                </Button>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          {note && (
            <p role={note.alert ? "alert" : "status"} className="text-xs">
              {note.text}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Sidebar.Group>
  );
}
import { Button, Input, Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CheckCircleIcon, ClockIcon, EyeIcon, TrashIcon, WarningIcon, XCircleIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { addWatch, deleteWatch, listWatches, notifyIncidentsChanged, WATCH_ERROR_TEXT, type Watch } from "../lib/api";

/** How often the panel reloads while the tab is visible: it shows each check's result and spots auto-opened incidents. */
const POLL_MS = 15_000;

/**
 * The watchlist (FR-40, TDD §12.1): add a URL, see each watch's last check, remove it. Cron checks them; when a
 * failure is confirmed an incident opens by itself, and this panel tells the incident list to reload.
 */
export function WatchlistPanel({ demoUrl }: { demoUrl?: string }) {
  const [watches, setWatches] = useState<Watch[] | null>(null);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const openIds = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const apply = useCallback((w: Watch[] | null) => {
    if (!w) return;
    setWatches(w);
    // A watch that just opened (or closed) an incident: the sidebar's list should show it now (polling is WP-19).
    const ids = w.map((x) => x.openIncidentId ?? "").join(",");
    if (openIds.current !== null && openIds.current !== ids) notifyIncidentsChanged();
    openIds.current = ids;
  }, []);
  const load = useCallback(() => listWatches().then(apply), [apply]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void listWatches().then(apply);
    const onVisible = () => document.visibilityState === "visible" && void listWatches().then(apply);
    const timer = setInterval(onVisible, POLL_MS);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
    };
  }, [apply]);

  const add = async (target: string) => {
    if (!target.trim() || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const r = await addWatch(target.trim());
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setError(r.message ?? WATCH_ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);
    setUrl("");
    await load();
  };

  const remove = async (w: Watch) => {
    setError(null);
    if (!(await deleteWatch(w.id))) return setError("Couldn't remove that watch. Try again.");
    await load();
  };

  const onSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    void add(url);
  };

  const demoWatched = !!demoUrl && !!watches?.some((w) => w.url === demoUrl);

  return (
    <Sidebar.Group>
      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>Watchlist</Sidebar.GroupLabel>
      {/* The sidebar keeps text on one line; errors and hints here need to wrap. */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 px-2 pb-2 break-words whitespace-normal">
        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex items-end gap-1.5">
          <Input
            label="Watch a URL"
            size="sm"
            value={url}
            onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="api.example.com"
            className="min-w-0 font-mono"
          />
          <Button type="submit" size="sm" icon={EyeIcon} loading={busy} disabled={!url.trim()}>
            Watch
          </Button>
        </form>
        {demoUrl && !demoWatched && (
          <Button size="xs" variant="ghost" className="w-fit" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void add(demoUrl)}>
            Watch my demo
          </Button>
        )}
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg)">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        {watches && watches.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Nothing watched yet. Checks run every few minutes for 24 hours.</p>}
        {watches && watches.length > 0 && (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5" aria-label="Watched URLs">
            {watches.map((w) => (
              <li key={w.id} className="flex items-start gap-1 rounded-md border border-(--cf-border) px-2 py-1.5">
                <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5">
                  <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs" title={w.url}>
                    {shortUrl(w.url)}
                  </span>
                  <WatchStatus watch={w} />
                  <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
                    {w.intervalSec <= 60 ? "Every minute" : `Every ${Math.round(w.intervalSec / 60)} min`} · {expiresIn(w.expiresAt)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Button
                  size="xs"
                  variant="ghost"
                  shape="square"
                  icon={TrashIcon}
                  aria-label={`Stop watching ${shortUrl(w.url)}`}
                  title="Stop watching"
                  onClick={() => void remove(w)}
                />
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </Sidebar.Group>
  );
}

/** Icon and words, never colour alone (TDD §12.4). */
function WatchStatus({ watch: w }: { watch: Watch }) {
  let icon: ReactNode;
  let text: ReactNode;
  if (w.openIncidentId) {
    icon = <WarningIcon aria-hidden size={14} />;
    text = (
      <Link href={`/i/${w.openIncidentId}`} className="underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange">
        Incident open
      </Link>
    );
  } else if (w.lastStatus === "fail") {
    icon = <XCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} />;
    text = "Last check failed";
  } else if (w.lastStatus === "pass") {
    icon = <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} />;
    text = "Passing";
  } else {
    icon = <ClockIcon aria-hidden size={14} />;
    text = "Not checked yet";
  }
  return (
    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
      {icon}
      {text}
    </span>
  );
}

export function shortUrl(url: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    return u.pathname.length > 1 ? `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}` : u.hostname;
  } catch {
    return url;
  }
}

export function expiresIn(expiresAt: number, now: number = Date.now()): string {
  const hours = Math.ceil((expiresAt - now) / 3_600_000);
  return hours <= 1 ? "ends within the hour" : `ends in ${hours} h`;
}
import { Banner, Checkbox, ClipboardText, Radio } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { ArrowSquareOutIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { DEMO_MODE_LABELS, DEMO_PROBLEMS, modesFrom, type DemoProblem } from "../../demo-service/src/modes";
import { DEMO_CHANGED, ERROR_TEXT, getDemo, setDemoModes, type Demo } from "../lib/api";

const SAVE_FAILED = "Couldn't change the mode. Try again.";
const NONE = "none";
type Choice = DemoProblem | typeof NONE;

/**
 * "Break the demo service" panel (FR-33, TDD §12.1). One main problem at a time (they change how the page
 * answers), plus an optional slow-down on top, so every combination stays visible to the diagnosis.
 */
export function DemoPanel() {
  const [demo, setDemo] = useState<Demo | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const load = () =>
      void getDemo().then((r) => {
        if (!live) return;
        if (r.ok) {
          setDemo(r.demo);
          setError(null);
        } else setError(ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);
      });
    load();
    window.addEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.removeEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);
    };
  }, []);

  const problem = DEMO_PROBLEMS.find((p) => demo?.modes.includes(p)) ?? null;
  const slow = demo?.modes.includes("slow") ?? false;

  const change = async (nextProblem: DemoProblem | null, nextSlow: boolean) => {
    if (!demo) return;
    const before = demo;
    const modes = modesFrom(nextProblem, nextSlow);
    setDemo({ ...demo, modes });
    setSaving(true);
    const r = await setDemoModes(modes);
    setSaving(false);
    if (r.ok) {
      setDemo(r.demo);
      setError(null);
    } else {
      setDemo(before);
      setError(r.error === "demo_unavailable" ? ERROR_TEXT.demo_unavailable : SAVE_FAILED);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="demo-panel-title" className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div>
        <h2 id="demo-panel-title" className="font-display text-base font-medium">
          Demo service
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Break your own demo site, then ask the copilot what's wrong.</p>
      </div>

      {demo && (
        <>
          <p className="text-sm" aria-live="polite">
            <span className="text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Now: </span>
            <span className="font-medium">{demo.modes.map((m) => DEMO_MODE_LABELS[m]).join(" + ")}</span>
          </p>

          <Radio.Group<Choice>
            legend="Problem"
            value={problem ?? NONE}
            onValueChange={(c) => void change(c === NONE ? null : c, slow)}
            disabled={saving}
          >
            <Radio.Item<Choice> value={NONE} label="None" />
            {DEMO_PROBLEMS.map((p) => (
              <Radio.Item<Choice> key={p} value={p} label={DEMO_MODE_LABELS[p]} />
            ))}
          </Radio.Group>

          <Checkbox label="Also slow (+3 s per response)" checked={slow} disabled={saving} onCheckedChange={(c) => void change(problem, c === true)} />

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Demo URL</span>
            <ClipboardText text={demo.url} className="w-full min-w-0" tooltip={{ text: "Copy URL", copiedText: "Copied" }} />
            <a
              href={demo.url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noreferrer"
              className="flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-md text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
            >
              Open the demo
              <ArrowSquareOutIcon aria-hidden size={14} />
            </a>
          </div>

          <p className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
            Then ask: <q className="text-(--cf-fg)">What's wrong with my demo service?</q>
          </p>
        </>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div role="alert">
          <Banner variant="error" description={error} />
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

export type Mode = "light" | "dark";

const QUERY = "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)";

type ModeSource = Pick<MediaQueryList, "matches" | "addEventListener" | "removeEventListener">;

/**
 * Mirrors the OS colour scheme onto `<html data-mode>`, which Kumo and our `light-dark()` tokens read.
 * Returns a cleanup function that stops listening.
 */
export function initTheme(
  root: HTMLElement = document.documentElement,
  media: ModeSource = window.matchMedia(QUERY),
): () => void {
  const apply = (dark: boolean) => {
    root.dataset.mode = dark ? "dark" : "light";
  };
  const onChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => apply(e.matches);

  apply(media.matches);
  media.addEventListener("change", onChange);
  return () => media.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
}
import { LinkProvider, Loader, Toasty, type LinkComponentProps } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { forwardRef, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { AppShell } from "./components/AppShell";
import { DemoPanel } from "./components/DemoPanel";
import { IncidentSidebar } from "./components/IncidentSidebar";
import { NotificationSettings } from "./components/NotificationSettings";
import { WatchlistPanel } from "./components/WatchlistPanel";
import { firstSession, getSession, INCIDENTS_CHANGED, type Session } from "./lib/api";
import { AppRoutes } from "./routes";

/** Kumo links (sidebar items, link buttons) navigate inside the app instead of reloading. */
const AppLink = forwardRef<HTMLAnchorElement, LinkComponentProps>(({ href, to, ...rest }, ref) => <Link ref={ref} href={href ?? to ?? "/"} {...rest} />);
AppLink.displayName = "AppLink";

export function App() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  // One session call creates the workspace cookie before anything else runs: each request without a cookie
  // would create its own workspace. If it fails the app still renders. Fetched again when incidents change,
  // so the budget banner appears without a reload once the guard trips.
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const load = () => void getSession().then((s) => live && s && setSession(s));
    void firstSession().then((s) => {
      if (!live) return;
      if (s) setSession(s);
      setReady(true);
    });
    window.addEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.removeEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <LinkProvider component={AppLink}>
      <Toasty>
        <AppShell
          sidebar={
            ready && (
              <>
                <IncidentSidebar />
                <WatchlistPanel demoUrl={session?.demoUrl} />
                <NotificationSettings />
              </>
            )
          }
          aside={ready && <DemoPanel />}
          budgetMode={session?.budgetMode}
        >
          {ready ? (
            <AppRoutes />
          ) : (
            <div className="m-auto flex items-center gap-2 p-8 text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
              <Loader size={16} /> Starting…
            </div>
          )}
        </AppShell>
      </Toasty>
    </LinkProvider>
  );
}
import { Route, Switch } from "wouter";
import { IncidentView } from "./components/IncidentView";
import { NewIncident } from "./components/NewIncident";
import { isId } from "../worker/lib/ids";

/** `/` starts an incident; `/i/:id` opens one (TDD §12). */
export function AppRoutes() {
  return (
    <Switch>
      <Route path="/" component={NewIncident} />
      <Route path="/i/:id">{(p) => (isId(p.id) ? <IncidentView key={p.id} id={p.id} /> : <NotFound />)}</Route>
      <Route component={NotFound} />
    </Switch>
  );
}

function NotFound() {
  return <p className="m-auto p-8 text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Page not found.</p>;
}
import { LinkButton, Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CaretRightIcon, GithubLogoIcon, PlusIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useMediaQuery } from "../hooks/useMediaQuery";
import { BudgetBanner } from "./BudgetBanner";

const REPO_URL = "https://github.com/sahilsaleem2907/cf_ai_incident_copilot";

type Props = {
  /** Incident list, watchlist and notification settings (WP-10, WP-18, WP-19). */
  sidebar?: ReactNode;
  /** Right column (DemoPanel). Below `lg` it collapses into a disclosure at the top of <main>. */
  aside?: ReactNode;
  children?: ReactNode;
  /** From `/api/session`: "rule_based" shows the BudgetBanner under the title bar. */
  budgetMode?: "ai" | "rule_based";
};

export function AppShell({ sidebar, aside, children, budgetMode }: Props) {
  // Mounted in one place only, so the panel keeps one copy of its state.
  const wide = useMediaQuery("(min-width: 1024px)");
  return (
    <div className="flex h-dvh flex-col">
      <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-200)">
        <div className="flex h-14 items-center justify-between px-4">
          <span className="font-display text-lg font-medium">Incident Copilot</span>
          <a
            href={REPO_URL}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
          >
            <GithubLogoIcon aria-hidden size={18} />
            GitHub
          </a>
        </div>
        {budgetMode === "rule_based" && <BudgetBanner />}
      </header>

      {/* Below 768px the sidebar becomes a sheet, opened from the trigger at the top of <main>. */}
      <Sidebar.Provider contained defaultOpen collapsible="offcanvas" mobileBreakpoint={768} className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <Sidebar aria-label="Incidents">
          <Sidebar.Header>
            <LinkButton href="/" variant="secondary" icon={PlusIcon} className="w-full">
              New incident
            </LinkButton>
          </Sidebar.Header>
          <Sidebar.Content>{sidebar}</Sidebar.Content>
        </Sidebar>

        <main className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto bg-(--cf-bg-100)">
          <div className="border-b border-(--cf-border) px-2 py-1 md:hidden">
            <Sidebar.Trigger aria-label="Show incidents" />
          </div>
          {!wide && aside && <NarrowAside>{aside}</NarrowAside>}
          {children}
        </main>

        {wide && aside && (
          <aside aria-label="Demo service" className="w-80 shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-200) p-4">
            {aside}
          </aside>
        )}
      </Sidebar.Provider>

      <footer className="shrink-0 border-t border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-200) px-4 py-2 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
        Built on Cloudflare
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The right column below `lg`: closed by default so the chat stays first. */
function NarrowAside({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <aside aria-label="Demo service" className="border-b border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-200)">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-controls="narrow-aside"
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
      >
        <CaretRightIcon aria-hidden size={12} className={open ? "rotate-90 transition-transform" : "transition-transform"} />
        Demo service
      </button>
      {/* The toggle already names the panel, so its own heading is kept for screen readers only. */}
      <div id="narrow-aside" hidden={!open} className="px-4 pb-4 [&_h2]:sr-only">
        {children}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
import { Badge, Loader } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CheckCircleIcon, HourglassIcon, MinusCircleIcon, WarningCircleIcon, XCircleIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import type { CheckView, IncidentStatus } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { STATUS_TEXT } from "../lib/incident";

type Variant = "success" | "error" | "warning" | "neutral" | "outline" | "info";

/** Check status with an icon **and** text, never colour alone (TDD §12.4). */
const CHECK: Record<CheckView["status"], { text: string; variant: Variant; icon: React.ReactNode }> = {
  pending: { text: "Pending", variant: "outline", icon: <HourglassIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },
  running: { text: "Running", variant: "info", icon: <Loader size={12} /> },
  pass: { text: "Pass", variant: "success", icon: <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight="fill" /> },
  fail: { text: "Fail", variant: "error", icon: <XCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight="fill" /> },
  error: { text: "Error", variant: "warning", icon: <WarningCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight="fill" /> },
  unavailable: { text: "Unavailable", variant: "neutral", icon: <MinusCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },
};

export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: CheckView["status"] }) {
  const s = CHECK[status];
  return (
    <Badge variant={s.variant} icon={s.icon}>
      {s.text}
    </Badge>
  );
}

const INCIDENT: Record<IncidentStatus, Variant> = {
  investigating: "info",
  awaiting_approval: "warning",
  mitigated: "outline",
  resolved: "success",
};

export function IncidentStatusBadge({ status }: { status: IncidentStatus }) {
  return <Badge variant={INCIDENT[status]}>{STATUS_TEXT[status]}</Badge>;
}

import type { IncidentStatus, RunStatus, TimelineView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { parseTarget } from "../../worker/safety/target";

/**
 * A follow-up that names a different host than the incident's (TDD §8.6). Tools are pinned to the incident's
 * origin (D6), so the UI offers a new incident instead of sending. "demo" alone doesn't count.
 */
export function otherHost(text: string, incidentHost: string): string | null {
  const r = parseTarget(text, "https://demo.invalid/");
  if (!r.ok || r.source === "demo") return null;
  return r.host === incidentHost.toLowerCase() ? null : r.host;
}

const LIMIT_NOTES: Partial<Record<RunStatus, string>> = {
  limit_turns: "Stopped at the 3-turn limit.",
  limit_tools: "Stopped at the 6-check limit.",
  limit_budget: "Stopped when this run's request budget ran out.",
  limit_deadline: "Stopped at the 60-second limit.",
};

/**
 * Says which limit ended the latest run (TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress"), or that it failed. Nothing while
 * a newer run is in progress, or when the run simply finished.
 */
export function runNote(timeline: readonly TimelineView[]): string | null {
  const last = [...timeline].reverse().find((t) => t.type === "run_finished" || t.type === "run_started");
  if (!last || last.type !== "run_finished") return null;
  const status = last.detail.status as RunStatus;
  if (status === "failed") return "This run failed before it finished. Try asking again.";
  const note = LIMIT_NOTES[status];
  return note ? `${note} The diagnosis uses the checks it completed.` : null;
}

export const STATUS_TEXT: Record<IncidentStatus, string> = {
  investigating: "Investigating",
  awaiting_approval: "Awaiting approval",
  mitigated: "Mitigated",
  resolved: "Resolved",
};

```
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import type { IncidentRow } from "../lib/api";
import { STATUS_TEXT } from "../lib/incident";

export type Permission = NotificationPermission | "unsupported";

const supported = () => typeof window !== "undefined" && "Notification" in window;
export const currentPermission = (): Permission => (supported() ? Notification.permission : "unsupported");

/** Browser notification permission (FR-51). It's only ever requested from a button click. */
export function useNotificationPermission(): [Permission, () => Promise<void>] {
  const [permission, setPermission] = useState<Permission>(currentPermission);
  const request = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!supported()) return;
    setPermission(await Notification.requestPermission());
  }, []);
  return [permission, request];
}

/** The notification's text: "<host> · <status>", plus "(watch)" for an incident a watch opened. */
export function notificationBody(row: Pick<IncidentRow, "target" | "status" | "origin">): string {
  let host = row.target;
  try {
    host = new URL(row.target).hostname;
  } catch {
    // keep the raw target
  }
  return `${host} · ${STATUS_TEXT[row.status]}${row.origin === "watch" ? " (watch)" : ""}`;
}

/** One notification per incident (`tag` replaces an older one); clicking it brings the tab back and opens it. */
export function showIncidentNotification(row: IncidentRow, open: (id: string) => void): void {
  if (currentPermission() !== "granted") return;
  const n = new Notification("Incident Copilot", { body: notificationBody(row), tag: row.id });
  n.onclick = () => {
    window.focus();
    open(row.id);
    n.close();
  };
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { newlyUnread } from "../../../web/hooks/useIncidents";
import { notificationBody } from "../../../web/hooks/useNotifications";
import { getWebhook, removeWebhook, saveWebhook, testWebhook, type IncidentRow } from "../../../web/lib/api";

const row = (id: string, unread: boolean, over: Partial<IncidentRow> = {}): IncidentRow => ({
  id,
  target: "https://api.example.com/",
  status: "investigating",
  origin: "watch",
  unread,
  updatedAt: 0,
  ...over,
});

describe("newlyUnread (D16)", () => {
  it("announces nothing on the first load", () => {
    expect(newlyUnread(null, [row("a", true)])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("announces rows that turned unread, and new unread rows, once", () => {
    const before = new Map([
      ["a", false],
      ["b", true],
    ]);
    const rows = [row("a", true), row("b", true), row("c", true), row("d", false)];
    expect(newlyUnread(before, rows).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a", "c"]);
    expect(newlyUnread(new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.unread])), rows)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

import { Button, Empty } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { FirstAidKitIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useRateLimitToast } from "../hooks/useRateLimitToast";
import { createIncident, ERROR_TEXT, notifyIncidentsChanged } from "../lib/api";
import { Composer } from "./Composer";

const CHIPS = [
  { label: "Use demo service", text: "What's wrong with my demo service?" },
  { label: "diagnose cloudflare.com", text: "diagnose cloudflare.com" },
];

/** Start screen: describe what's broken; the first message must name a host, URL or "demo" (D9). */
export function NewIncident() {
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const rateLimited = useRateLimitToast();

  const send = async (message: string) => {
    setError(null);
    const r = await createIncident(message);
    if (!r.ok) {
      setError(r.error === "rate_limited" ? rateLimited(r.retryAfter) : ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);
      return;
    }
    setText("");
    notifyIncidentsChanged();
    navigate(`/i/${r.id}`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-full shrink-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center p-6">
        <Empty
          icon={<FirstAidKitIcon size={40} aria-hidden />}
          title="What's broken?"
          description="Name a site and describe the problem. Checks run on DNS and HTTP, then you get a diagnosis with evidence."
          contents={
            <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
              {CHIPS.map((c) => (
                <Button key={c.label} variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setText(c.text)}>
                  {c.label}
                </Button>
              ))}
            </div>
          }
        />
      </div>
      <Composer
        value={text}
        onChange={setText}
        onSend={send}
        placeholder="e.g. checkout on shop.example.com is returning errors"
        notice={error && <p role="alert" className="text-(--cf-fg)">{error}</p>}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

Narrow screens: one drop-down \"Demo & watchlist\" |\n| Q9 | Header: **orange cloud mark** + muted \"×\" + \"Incident Copilot\" |\n| Q10 | Faint **dot grid behind the main area only**, plus a **soft warm glow at the bottom of the New incident page only** |\n| Q11 | \"x new\" badge = light orange pill. ## Approved Plan:
# WP-27 UI polish: orange CTA, status colours, header, right bar, background

## Context
The UI works, but it looks plain and is a bit inconsistent: "New incident" is a grey button, statuses are grey text in the
sidebar and Kumo's default colours on the page, browser notifications and the watchlist sit low in the left sidebar,
and the header is text only. The user wants a cleaner Cloudflare look (based on cloudflare.com's hero: orange, dotted
grid, warm glow) and **one colour language for statuses across the app**. Every decision below was agreed in the grilling session.

Note: two earlier TDD rules change on purpose. §12.4 said "orange only for main actions"; §12.1 said "no Cloudflare logo".
Both get updated and logged.

## Decisions (agreed)
| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| Q1 | Status colours: Investigating = **orange**, Awaiting approval = **purple**, Mitigated = **grey**, Resolved = **green**. Checks: Running orange, Pass green, Fail red, Error yellow, Pending/Unavailable grey. Watchlist: Passing green, Last check failed red, Incident open orange, Not checked grey |
| Q2 | **Soft pill everywhere** (light tinted background, coloured text, icon + words) on the incident page and every sidebar row |
| Q3 | New reusable **pill-shaped orange `CtaButton`** (button or link) |
| Q4 | Browser notifications move to the **header**, left of GitHub. Slack/Discord webhook stays in the **left sidebar** |
| Q5/Q6 | Header control is a **Switch**. First switch-on asks the browser; after that on/off is an app setting saved in this browser. Blocked → switch greyed with tooltip "Blocked in browser settings". Unsupported → hidden. Label "Notifications" on desktop, icon + switch on phones |
| Q7 | Slack + Discord logos in a row as the webhook section title. **Grey by default; the saved one shows in brand colour** |
| Q8 | Right bar = **Demo service**, a **dashed divider**, then **Watchlist**, each with a small uppercase label. Narrow screens: one drop-down "Demo & watchlist" |
| Q9 | Header: **orange cloud mark** + muted "×" + "Incident Copilot" |
| Q10 | Faint **dot grid behind the main area only**, plus a **soft warm glow at the bottom of the New incident page only** |
| Q11 | "x new" badge = light orange pill. Unread row = **orange dot + bold host**, visible word "New" dropped (kept as screen-reader text) |
| Q12 | Use the logo, **keep** the "Built on Cloudflare" footer, record the change in TDD + TASKS log |

## Changes

### 1. Design tokens — `web/styles.css`
- Status tones as `light-dark()` tokens: `--status-{orange,purple,green,red,yellow,grey}-fg` / `-bg`. Base them on Kumo's
  tokens where they exist (`--color-kumo-success*`, `--color-kumo-danger*`, `--color-kumo-warning*`, `--color-kumo-badge-purple`),
  orange from `--color-cf-orange`. Tint bg via `color-mix(in oklab, <fg> 14%, transparent)`.
- `.bg-dot-grid`: `radial-gradient` dots (~1px dot, ~8px spacing) in a faint `light-dark()` colour, low contrast in both modes.
- `.bg-glow`: radial warm ellipse at the bottom using `--color-cf-orange` at low alpha (lower in dark mode).
- `.divider-dashed`: dashed 1px line that matches the dot grid (repeating-linear-gradient so dash length/gap match the grid).
- No `dark:` variants (TDD §12.4 rule stays).

### 2. One status colour map — new `web/lib/status.ts`
- `type Tone = "orange" | "purple" | "green" | "red" | "yellow" | "grey"`.
- `INCIDENT_TONE: Record<IncidentStatus, Tone>`, `CHECK_TONE: Record<CheckView["status"], Tone>`, `WATCH_TONE` (for the
  4 watch states in `WatchlistPanel.WatchStatus`). Text stays in `STATUS_TEXT` (`web/lib/incident.ts`).
- `ApprovalCard` badge (`web/components/ApprovalCard.tsx`) maps onto the same tones: waiting = purple, approved/verified = green,
  still failing/failed = red, working = orange, rejected/expired/superseded = grey.

### 3. `StatusPill` — rewrite `web/components/StatusBadge.tsx`
- New `StatusPill({ tone, icon, children, size? })`: rounded-full span using the tone tokens (same base classes as Kumo's
  `KUMO_BADGE_BASE_STYLES`). Icon **and** text always (TDD §12.4).
- `StatusBadge` (checks) and `IncidentStatusBadge` keep their names/props, but render `StatusPill` with the tone maps.
  Add an icon per incident status (e.g. spinner-ish/Hourglass for investigating, HandPalm/UserCheck for awaiting, ShieldCheck for mitigated, CheckCircle for resolved).

### 4. `CtaButton` — new `web/components/CtaButton.tsx`
- `CtaButton` = Kumo `Button` with `variant="primary"` + `rounded-full`; `CtaLinkButton` = Kumo `LinkButton` same styling.
  Same props as Kumo's (pass-through), so it drops in anywhere.
- Use it for: New incident (`AppShell.tsx`), Send (`Composer.tsx`), Approve (`ApprovalCard.tsx`), Watch (`WatchlistPanel.tsx`),
  Save webhook (webhook settings).

### 5. Header — `web/components/AppShell.tsx`
- Left: new `web/components/CloudflareMark.tsx` (inline two-tone orange cloud SVG, `aria-hidden`, ~22px) + muted "×" +
  "Incident Copilot", wrapped in a link to `/`, accessible name "Incident Copilot".
- Right: `NotificationToggle` then GitHub (GitHub becomes icon-only below `sm`, keeps `aria-label`).

### 6. Notifications switch — new `web/components/NotificationToggle.tsx`, edit `web/hooks/useNotifications.ts`
- Pure helper `notificationsOn(permission, stored: "on" | "off" | null)`: on only if permission is `granted` and stored isn't
  `"off"` (so people who already allowed them stay on).
- Hook `useNotificationSetting()` → `{ permission, on, setOn }`. `setOn(true)` with permission `default` calls
  `Notification.requestPermission()` (still only from a click, FR-51). Stored in `localStorage` (`notifications`), every
  read/write in try/catch.
- `showIncidentNotification` also checks the stored setting.
- UI: Kumo `Switch` + `BellIcon`/`BellSlashIcon`; label "Notifications" hidden below `sm` (kept for screen readers);
  `Tooltip` "Blocked in browser settings" when denied (switch disabled); nothing rendered when unsupported.

### 7. Webhook section — rename `NotificationSettings.tsx` → `WebhookSettings.tsx` (the TDD's name)
- Remove the "Browser" block (moved to header).
- Title row: `SlackLogoIcon` + `DiscordLogoIcon` (Phosphor) side by side, then "Slack or Discord" (or "Discord · …hint" once saved).
  Both grey (`--cf-fg-muted`); when `hook.webhook.kind` is set, that logo uses its brand colour (Discord `#5865F2`;
  Slack aubergine `#4A154B` light / `#E01E5A` dark via `light-dark()`).
- Save webhook → `CtaButton`.

### 8. Sidebar list — `web/components/IncidentSidebar.tsx`
- "x new" → `StatusPill tone="orange"` (light orange).
- Unread row: orange dot (`bg-cf-orange`) + bold host; visible "New ·" removed, `<span className="sr-only">New</span>` kept.
- Second line: `IncidentStatusBadge` (small) + grey "· 👁 Watch" when `origin === "watch"`.
- Left sidebar now = New incident (CTA) → Incidents → Webhook settings (`App.tsx`).

### 9. Right bar — `App.tsx`, `AppShell.tsx`, `WatchlistPanel.tsx`, `DemoPanel.tsx`
- `aside` = `<DemoPanel />` + `<hr className="divider-dashed" />` + `<WatchlistPanel demoUrl=… />`.
- `WatchlistPanel` stops using `Sidebar.Group`/`GroupLabel` (no Sidebar context in the aside) → plain `<section aria-labelledby>`
  with the same small uppercase label style as Demo service. `WatchStatus` renders `StatusPill` with `WATCH_TONE`.
- `DemoPanel` heading becomes the same small uppercase section label.
- `NarrowAside`: button text and `aria-label` "Demo & watchlist"; wide `<aside aria-label>` the same.

### 10. Background
- `<main>` in `AppShell.tsx` gets `bg-dot-grid` (sidebars stay plain). Check cards/composer keep their own solid backgrounds so text stays readable.
- `NewIncident.tsx` outer div gets `bg-glow`.

### 11. Docs and tracking (per memory rules)
- **TDD.md** §12.1 (header with logo + notifications switch; left sidebar = incidents + WebhookSettings; right column = Demo service + Watchlist; "Demo & watchlist" drop-down), §12.2 (status colour map table, StatusPill, CtaButton), §12.4 (orange now means: main actions, highlight rings, in-progress/"investigating" status, unread).
- **TASKS.md**: add WP-27 "UI polish" row (Started/Done times), update "Now", add decisions-log rows (logo rule override; orange rule change; app-level notification switch because sites can't revoke browser permission).
- **PROMPTS.md**: append this prompt + all 12 Q&A answers.
- Work on `dev`; user raises the dev → main PR.

## Tests
- New `test/unit/web/status.test.ts`: every incident/check/watch status has a tone; the agreed ones are pinned (investigating orange, awaiting purple, mitigated grey, resolved green, pass green, fail red).
- Extend `test/unit/web/notifications.test.ts`: `notificationsOn` for granted/denied/default/unsupported × on/off/null.
- `test/unit/web/watchlist.test.ts` still imports `shortUrl`/`expiresIn` from `WatchlistPanel` — keep those exports.

## Verification
1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`.
2. `pnpm dev`, then in the browser (light **and** dark mode):
   - Header: cloud × Incident Copilot, Notifications switch, GitHub. Switch on → browser prompt → on; off → break the demo with a watch and confirm no browser notification; on again → one appears.
   - New incident button is an orange pill; Send/Approve/Watch/Save webhook match.
   - Sidebar: light-orange "x new", unread orange dot + bold, status pills in the right colours; Slack/Discord logos grey, saved one coloured.
   - Right bar: Demo service, dashed divider, Watchlist; "Watch my demo" still works; watch pills coloured.
   - Dot grid behind main only; glow on `/` only.
   - Resize to < 1024px ("Demo & watchlist" drop-down) and < 768px (sidebar sheet, icon-only header items, no horizontal scroll).

```

(2) **`run_finished` records `source` (`model` / `model+rules` / `rules`) and `fallbackTurns`** (user decision), so results say who got it right; no schema change. (3) A `rule_based` run fails whatever it found. (4) ③ needs an approval card for broken modes; for healthy, no card and no restore in the findings. (5) A 429 waits `retryAfter`. **Result:** 5/5, 642 neurons; `redirect_loop` was `model+rules` (an invalid `submit_findings`; the reason isn't stored, so it's unknown from this run). Check ③ is mostly guaranteed by `pickAction` (WP-12). | 2026-10-15 23:56 | 19 | **Notifications as built.** (1) The "opened" webhook waits for the first diagnosis (up to 9 × 10 s) so it names the likely cause (user decision). (2) Mitigated/resolved webhooks for **any incident with a watch attached**, including chat incidents with an approved "Add to watchlist" (user decision, widens D18). (3) Migration `0002` adds `webhooks.app_origin`, recorded on save, for incident links without committing the URL (D2). (4) `POST /api/webhook/test` (5 an hour) so a user can check the URL. (5) `WEBHOOK_KEY` isn't declared in wrangler: without it webhooks are off and the UI says so; the **user sets it in production** (user decision). (6) Automatic status changes set unread; `IncidentView` marks read on open and on new activity while visible. (7) `GET /api/webhook` returns `{available, webhook}`. **Local check (5181; the old 5173 server is still broken):** an auto-opened watch incident showed "New" + "1 new" 40 s after the cron check, raised exactly one browser notification (stubbed Notification API), and cleared on open; light, dark and 390 px screenshots. The local `.dev.vars` key wasn't added (the safety check gave no verdict on writing a secret), so the save/test UI wasn't run locally; the integration tests cover it. **Folded into TDD §5.1, §6.1, §7.1, §11, §12.3, §14 and the README.** | The PRD wants the diagnosis ready when the user is notified. A user who asked to watch an incident wants to hear when it recovers |

### 23:45 · WP-19 plan

> Ive tested the demo walkthrough its worked. Lets build WP-19.

**Claude asked:** when should the "opened" Slack/Discord message go out, given the diagnosis is still running?
**Answer:** after the first diagnosis (at most about 90 s), so it carries the likely cause.

**Claude asked:** should chat incidents that became watched (approved "Add to watchlist") also send webhooks when they recover?
**Answer:** yes, any incident with a watch attached.

**Claude asked:** who sets the production `WEBHOOK_KEY` secret?
**Answer:** the user runs a one-line command Claude provides.

(Plan approved: sidebar polling every 10 s with unread dots and `markRead`, browser notifications from a button, Slack/Discord webhooks encrypted with AES-GCM, and `NotifyWorkflow` sending at most once per state change.)

(WP-19 built: sidebar polling with unread markers, browser notifications, Slack/Discord webhooks sent by `NotifyWorkflow`. 585 tests pass. The fake-Discord integration test gets exactly one message per state change. Locally, a watch failure showed as "New" and raised one browser notification. Webhooks go live once the user sets `WEBHOOK_KEY` on the deployed Worker.)

import { Button } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { PaperPlaneRightIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState, type KeyboardEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";

type Props = {
  onSend: (text: string) => Promise<void>;
  value?: string;
  onChange?: (text: string) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** Shown under the box when disabled (e.g. "Working on it…"). */
  disabledReason?: string;
  placeholder: string;
  /** Inline error or offer shown above the box. */
  notice?: ReactNode;
};

/** Enter sends, Shift+Enter adds a new line (TDD §12.4). Controlled or uncontrolled. */
export function Composer({ onSend, value, onChange, disabled, disabledReason, placeholder, notice }: Props) {
  const [own, setOwn] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const text = value ?? own;
  const setText = onChange ?? setOwn;
  const off = disabled || busy;

  const submit = async () => {
    const t = text.trim();
    if (!t || off) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await onSend(t);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
      e.preventDefault();
      void submit();
    }
  };

  return (
    <form
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 pb-4"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        void submit();
      }}
    >
      {notice && <div className="mb-2 text-sm">{notice}</div>}
      <div className="flex items-end gap-2 rounded-lg border border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-200) p-2 focus-within:border-cf-orange">
        <label htmlFor="composer" className="sr-only">
          Message
        </label>
        <textarea
          id="composer"
          rows={2}
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={disabled && disabledReason ? "composer-reason" : undefined}
          className="max-h-48 min-h-11 flex-1 resize-y bg-transparent px-2 py-1.5 outline-none placeholder:text-(--cf-fg-muted) disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        />
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" icon={PaperPlaneRightIcon} disabled={off || !text.trim()} loading={busy}>
          Send
        </Button>
      </div>
      {disabled && disabledReason && (
        <p id="composer-reason" className="mt-1 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
          {disabledReason}
        </p>
      )}
    </form>
  );
}
import { Badge, Button, Loader, Surface } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CheckCircleIcon, HourglassIcon, MinusCircleIcon, ShieldCheckIcon, XCircleIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { ApprovalView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { cardState, whyText, type CardState } from "../lib/approval";
import { Markdown } from "./Markdown";

const BADGE: Record<CardState["tone"], { variant: "warning" | "info" | "success" | "error" | "neutral"; icon: React.ReactNode }> = {
  waiting: { variant: "warning", icon: <HourglassIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },
  working: { variant: "info", icon: <Loader size={12} /> },
  good: { variant: "success", icon: <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight="fill" /> },
  bad: { variant: "error", icon: <XCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight="fill" /> },
  closed: { variant: "neutral", icon: <MinusCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },
};

type Props = {
  approval: ApprovalView;
  knownIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  /** Owner only (D8): viewers see the card without buttons. */
  onDecide?: (id: string, decision: "approve" | "reject") => Promise<unknown>;
};

/** A proposed action waiting for a human (FR-20, TDD §12.2). Nothing runs until Approve is clicked. */
export function ApprovalCard({ approval, knownIds, onDecide }: Props) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<"approve" | "reject" | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const state = cardState(approval);
  const badge = BADGE[state.tone];
  const titleId = `approval-${approval.id}`;

  const decide = async (decision: "approve" | "reject") => {
    if (!onDecide) return;
    setBusy(decision);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onDecide(approval.id, decision);
    } catch {
      setError("That didn't go through. Try again.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Surface
      role="group"
      aria-labelledby={titleId}
      className={`rounded-lg border p-4 ${approval.status === "pending" ? "border-cf-orange" : "border-(--cf-border)"}`}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <ShieldCheckIcon aria-hidden size={18} className="text-(--cf-fg-muted)" />
        <span className="text-xs tracking-wide text-(--cf-fg-muted) uppercase">Proposed action</span>
        <Badge variant={badge.variant} icon={badge.icon}>
          {state.text}
        </Badge>
      </div>
      <h3 id={titleId} className="mt-2 font-medium">
        {approval.title}
      </h3>
      {approval.cause && (
        <div className="mt-2 text-sm">
          <span className="font-medium">Why: </span>
          <Markdown text={whyText(approval)} knownIds={knownIds} />
        </div>
      )}
      {approval.outcome && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{approval.outcome.text}</p>}

      {approval.status === "pending" && onDecide && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="primary" icon={CheckCircleIcon} loading={busy === "approve"} disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => void decide("approve")}>
            Approve
          </Button>
          <Button variant="secondary" icon={XCircleIcon} loading={busy === "reject"} disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => void decide("reject")}>
            Reject
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
      {approval.status === "pending" && !onDecide && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Only the incident's owner can approve this.</p>}
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </Surface>
  );
}
    setText("");
    notifyIncidentsChanged();
    navigate(`/i/${r.id}`);
  };

  return (
    // shrink-0: as a flex child of the scrolling <main> it would otherwise shrink to one screen, and the
    // sticky header and composer would scroll away with it.
    <div className="flex min-h-full shrink-0 flex-col">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-100) px-4 py-3">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-3xl flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <h1 className="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-base">{state.target}</h1>
          <IncidentStatusBadge status={state.status} />
          {state.category && <span className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">· {CATEGORY_TEXT[state.category] ?? state.category}</span>}
          {viewer && (
            <Badge variant="neutral" icon={<EyeIcon aria-hidden size={14} />} className="ml-auto">
              View only
            </Badge>
          )}
          {role === "owner" && state.status !== "resolved" && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="secondary"
              icon={CheckCircleIcon}
              className="ml-auto"
              loading={resolving}
              onClick={() => {
                setResolving(true);
                resolve()
                  .catch(() => setError("Couldn't resolve the incident. Try again."))
                  .finally(() => setResolving(false));
              }}
            >
              Resolve
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
        {state.monitor && (
          <p className="mx-auto mt-1 flex max-w-3xl items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)" aria-live="polite">
            <PulseIcon aria-hidden size={14} />
            Watching after the fix: {Math.min(state.monitor.passes, MONITOR_PASSES)} of {MONITOR_PASSES} checks passed in a row · {state.monitor.ticksLeft} checks left
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto mt-3 flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-2 px-4">
        {viewer && <ReadOnlyBanner />}
        {state.mode === "rule_based" && (
          <Banner variant="alert" icon={<RobotIcon aria-hidden />} title="AI unavailable, rule-based diagnosis" description="The checks ran and fixed rules picked the diagnosis." />
        )}
        {note && !state.activeRunId && <Banner variant="secondary" icon={<InfoIcon aria-hidden />} description={note} />}

\"gap-0.5 px-1.5 text-[11px] leading-4\" : \"gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs\";\n  return (\n    <span className={`status-pill tone-${tone} inline-flex w-fit shrink-0 items-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap ${sizing} ${className}`}>\n      {icon}\n      {children}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Check status with an icon **and** text, never colour alone (TDD §12.4). */\nconst CHECK: Record<CheckView[\"status\"], { text: string; icon: ReactNode }> = {\n  pending: { text: \"Pending\", icon: <HourglassIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },\n  running: { text: \"Running\", icon: <Loader size={12} /> },\n  pass: { text: \"Pass\", icon: <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight=\"fill\" /> },\n  fail: { text: \"Fail\", icon: <XCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight=\"fill\" /> },\n  error: { text: \"Error\", icon: <WarningCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} weight=\"fill\" /> },\n  unavailable: { text: \"Unavailable\", icon: <MinusCircleIcon aria-hidden size={14} /> },\n};\n\nexport function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: CheckView[\"status\"] }) {\n  const s = CHECK[status];\n  return (\n    <StatusPill tone={CHECK_TONE[status]} icon={s.icon}>\n      {s.text}\n    </StatusPill>\n  );\n}\n\nconst INCIDENT_ICON: Record<IncidentStatus, (size: number) => ReactNode> = {\n  investigating: (size) => <MagnifyingGlassIcon aria-hidden size={size} weight=\"bold\" />,\n  awaiting_approval: (size) => <HandPalmIcon aria-hidden size={size} weight=\"fill\" />,\n  mitigated: (size) => <ShieldCheckIcon aria-hidden size={size} weight=\"fill\" />,\n  resolved: (size) => <CheckCircleIcon aria-hidden size={size} weight=\"fill\" />,\n};\n\nexport function IncidentStatusBadge({ status, size = \"base\" }: { status: IncidentStatus; size?: \"sm\" | \"base\" }) {\n  return (\n    <StatusPill tone={INCIDENT_TONE[status]} size={size} icon={INCIDENT_ICON[status](size === \"sm\" ? 11 : 14)}>\n      {STATUS_TEXT[status]}\n    </StatusPill>\n  );\n}\n"

Notification.permission : \"unsupported\");\n\n/** Notifications show only with the browser's permission and the switch not turned off (people who allowed them before stay on). */\nexport const notificationsOn = (permission: Permission, stored: Stored): boolean => permission === \"granted\" && stored !== \"off\";\n\nfunction readStored(): Stored {\n  try {\n    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);\n    return v === \"on\" || v === \"off\" ? v : null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction writeStored(v: \"on\" | \"off\"): void {\n  try {\n    localStorage.setItem(KEY, v);\n  } catch {\n    // private mode or blocked storage: the switch still works for this page\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * The header's notifications switch (FR-51). Switching on the first time asks the browser, and only from that\n * click; after that it's the app's own setting. Permission is re-read on focus, in case it changed in browser settings.\n */\nexport function useNotificationSetting(): { permission: Permission; on: boolean; setOn: (on: boolean) => Promise<void> } {\n  const [permission, setPermission] = useState<Permission>(currentPermission);\n  const [stored, setStored] = useState<Stored>(readStored);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const sync = () => setPermission(currentPermission());\n    window.addEventListener(\"focus\", sync);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"focus\", sync);\n  }, []);\n\n  const setOn = useCallback(async (on: boolean) => {\n    if (on && supported() && Notification.permission === \"default\") {\n      const p = await Notification.requestPermission();\n      setPermission(p);\n      if (p !== \"granted\") return;\n    }\n    const v = on ? \"on\" : \"off\";\n    writeStored(v);\n    setStored(v);\n  }, []);\n\n  return { permission, on: notificationsOn(permission, stored), setOn };\n}\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new)\ns=s.replace('  if (currentPermission() !== \"granted\") return;','  if (!notificationsOn(currentPermission(), readStored())) return;')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ngrep -rn \"useNotificationPermission\" web test",

}

  "content": "import { Switch, Tooltip } from \"@cloudflare/kumo\";\nimport { BellIcon, BellSlashIcon } from \"@phosphor-icons/react\";\nimport { useNotificationSetting } from \"../hooks/useNotifications\";\n\n/**\n * Browser notifications on/off, in the header (FR-51, TDD §12.1). Blocked in the browser: greyed out with the\n * reason in a tooltip. Not supported: not shown. The word \"Notifications\" is visible from `sm` up.\n */\nexport function NotificationToggle() {\n  const { permission, on, setOn } = useNotificationSetting();\n  if (permission === \"unsupported\") return null;\n  const blocked = permission === \"denied\";\n\n  const control = (\n    <Switch\n      size=\"sm\"\n      controlFirst={false}\n      checked={on}\n      disabled={blocked}\n      onCheckedChange={(c) => void setOn(c)}\n      label={\n        <span className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm ${blocked ? \"text-(--cf-fg-muted)\" : \"\"}`}>\n          {blocked ? <BellSlashIcon aria-hidden size={16} /> : <BellIcon aria-hidden size={16} />}\n          <span className=\"max-sm:sr-only\">Notifications</span>\n          {blocked && <span className=\"sr-only\">(blocked in browser settings)</span>}\n        </span>\n      }\n    />\n  );\n\n  return blocked ? (\n    <Tooltip content=\"Blocked in browser settings\">\n      <span>{control}</span>\n    </Tooltip>\n  ) : (\n    control\n  );\n}\n"
}

WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT[r.error], alert: true });\n  };\n\n  const save = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!url.trim()) return;\n    void run(\n      () => saveWebhook(url.trim()),\n      (info) => {\n        setUrl(\"\");\n        setHook({ available: true, webhook: info });\n        setNote({ text: \"Saved. Watched incidents will post here.\", alert: false });\n      },\n    );\n  };\n\n  const kind = hook?.webhook?.kind;\n  const logoColor = (k: keyof typeof KIND_TEXT) => (kind === k ? `var(--brand-${k})` : \"var(--cf-fg-muted)\");\n\n  return (\n    <Sidebar.Group>\n      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>Notifications</Sidebar.GroupLabel>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5 px-2 pb-3 break-words whitespace-normal\">\n        <span className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium\">\n          <span aria-hidden className=\"flex items-center gap-0.5\">\n            <SlackLogoIcon size={18} weight=\"fill\" style={{ color: logoColor(\"slack\") }} />\n            <DiscordLogoIcon size={18} weight=\"fill\" style={{ color: logoColor(\"discord\") }} />\n          </span>\n          {kind ? KIND_TEXT[kind] : \"Slack or Discord\"}\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">Posts when a watched site breaks, recovers and is resolved.</span>\n        {hook && !hook.available && <span className=\"text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">{WEBHOOK_ERROR_TEXT.webhooks_unavailable}</span>}\n        {hook?.available && !hook.webhook && (\n          <form onSubmit={save} className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n            <SensitiveInput label=\"Webhook URL\" size=\"sm\" value={url} onValueChange={setUrl} placeholder=\"https://discord.com/api/webhooks/…\" />\n            <CtaButton type=\"submit\" size=\"sm\" className=\"w-fit\" loading={busy} disabled={!url.trim()}>\n              Save webhook\n            </CtaButton>\n          </form>\n        )}\n        {hook?.webhook && (\n          <>\n            <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{hook.webhook.hint}</span>\n            <div className=\"flex gap-1.5\">\n              <Button size=\"xs\" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(testWebhook, () => setNote({ text: \"Test message sent.\", alert: false }))}>\n                Send test\n              </Button>\n              <Button\n                size=\"xs\"\n                variant=\"secondary-destructive\"\n                disabled={busy}\n                onClick={() => void run(removeWebhook, () => setHook({ available: true, webhook: null }))}\n              >\n                Remove\n              </Button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n        {note && (\n          <p role={note.alert ? \"alert\" : \"status\"} className=\"text-xs\">\n            {note.text}\n          </p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </Sidebar.Group>\n  );\n}\n"
}

describe("webhook API client", () => {
  const fake = (status: number, body?: unknown) => (async () => new Response(body === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(body), { status })) as unknown as typeof fetch;

  it("maps results and errors", async () => {
    expect(await getWebhook(fake(200, { available: false, webhook: null }))).toEqual({ ok: true, value: { available: false, webhook: null } });
    expect(await saveWebhook("x", fake(400, { error: "invalid_webhook", message: "Only Slack…" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalid_webhook", message: "Only Slack…" });
    expect(await saveWebhook("x", fake(503, { error: "webhooks_unavailable" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "webhooks_unavailable" });
    expect(await testWebhook(fake(429, { error: "rate_limited", retryAfter: 60 }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "rate_limited" });
    expect(await testWebhook(fake(502, { error: "send_failed" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "send_failed" });
    expect(await removeWebhook(fake(204))).toEqual({ ok: true, value: null });
    expect(await getWebhook(fake(500, { error: "boom" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "server" });
  });
});

    expect(expiresIn(now + 24 * 3_600_000, now)).toBe("ends in 24 h");

node      80033 sahil   59u  IPv6 0x533c5533630cec02      0t0  TCP localhost:5173 (LISTEN)
# Example: Browser-driven web app

You have a dev server that serves HTML to a browser. An agent in a
headless container can't open a browser window - so "run the app" means
launching the dev server, driving a headless Chromium against it, and
producing a screenshot that proves the page rendered.

Don't write a browser driver. Use `chromium-cli`.

## Dev server

Find the dev command (`package.json` `scripts.dev`, `Makefile`,
README), start it in the background, and wait for it to actually serve:

```bash
npm run dev &   # or yarn dev, pnpm dev, make serve, ./dev.sh
timeout 30 bash -c 'until curl -sf http://localhost:3000 >/dev/null; do sleep 1; done'
```

Don't `sleep 5` - poll the port. Stop by killing the port's listener
-- `lsof -ti:3000 -sTCP:LISTEN | xargs -r kill` - before relaunching,
or the next run hits `EADDRINUSE`. (`$!` after `npm run dev &` is only
the npm wrapper; npm doesn't forward SIGTERM to the server it spawned,
so the port kill is what actually frees it.) Avoid `pkill -f` with a
broad pattern - it can match the agent's own command line and kill the
session.

## Drive

`chromium-cli` is a headless-Chromium REPL. Pipe a script to stdin:

```bash
chromium-cli --session app <<'EOF'
nav http://localhost:3000
wait-for text=Dashboard
screenshot
click button:has-text("New item")
fill input[name="title"] Smoke test
press Enter
wait-for text=Smoke test
screenshot
console --errors
EOF
```

Screenshots land in `chromium_cli/sessions/app/screenshots/` (latest
symlinked as `screenshot.png`). That's the whole loop: `nav` ->
`wait-for` the element you need -> act (`click` / `fill` / `type` /
`press`) -> `screenshot` -> `console --errors` to check nothing threw.
Full command reference: `chromium-cli` skill, or `help` at the prompt.

For iterative debugging, run it under tmux and `send-keys` one command
at a time - same commands, same session.

**If `chromium-cli` isn't available:** adapt
[electron.md](electron.md)'s REPL driver - the structure and commands
transfer, but it's `_electron`-specific:
import `{ chromium }` instead, launch with
`chromium.launch({ args: ['--no-sandbox'] })`, acquire the page via
`(await app.newContext()).newPage()` then `goto()` your dev URL, and
````

</details>

### 🤖 Claude · 02:27:12

(That "failed" notice is just the dev server I stopped.) Updating the TDD.

**Tool call: `Read`**

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/sahil/personal/cloudflare-infra-chatbot/TDD.md",
  "offset": 700,
  "limit": 85
}
```

<details><summary>Tool result</summary>

````
700	
701	### 12.1 Layout (`AppShell`)
702	
703	- **Header:** wordmark "Incident Copilot", `BudgetBanner`, and a GitHub link. Don't use the Cloudflare logo; "Built on Cloudflare" in the footer is enough.
704	- **Left `Sidebar`:** "New incident" and the incident list (status badge, unread dot, polled every 10 s), followed by `WatchlistPanel` and `WebhookSettings`.
705	- **Main area:** `ChatPane`.
706	- **Right column:** `DemoPanel`. Below the `lg` breakpoint it becomes a closed "Demo service" disclosure at the top of `<main>` (an `aria-expanded` button, as for evidence). `useMediaQuery` mounts it in one place only, so the panel keeps a single state.
707	
708	### 12.2 Components
709	
710	- **`ChatPane`:** messages, check cards inline in run order, approval cards, and a composer that's disabled while `activeRunId` is set. The empty state shows chips for "Use demo service" and "diagnose cloudflare.com". The composer includes `VoiceButton`.
711	- **`CheckCard`:** tool icon, label, and a `StatusBadge` with icon **and** text (`Pending`, `Running` with a `Loader`, `✓ Pass`, `✕ Fail`, `Unavailable`). It shows a summary and expands with `Collapsible` to show evidence in `Code`, which is fetched lazily with `getEvidence`. Clicking an `[http-1]` chip scrolls to the card and flashes an orange ring.
712	- **`ApprovalCard`:** the action title, why it's proposed (cause plus refs), and **Approve** (orange primary) and **Reject** buttons. It shows the resolved state: approved, rejected, expired or superseded. For viewers, the buttons are hidden and `ReadOnlyBanner` is shown.
713	  - *As built (WP-12):* the card sits right after its run's narration in the feed. Its status badge reads, in words:
714	    - Waiting for your approval;
715	    - Approved, working on it… / checking it…;
716	    - Approved and verified;
717	    - Approved, but still failing / the action failed;
718	    - Rejected;
719	    - Expired, no action taken;
720	    - Replaced by a newer proposal.
721	
722	    A pending card has an orange border. Viewers see "Only the incident's owner can approve this."
723	  - The header has a **Resolve** button (owner, not resolved) and a "Watching after the fix: n of 3" line while the monitor runs.
724	  - A finished restore fires `demo:changed`, so the `DemoPanel` reloads.
725	- **Status chip in the incident header:** `investigating`, `awaiting approval`, `mitigated` or `resolved`, always with text. It's followed by Resolve and Postmortem buttons.
726	
727	### 12.3 State
728	
729	`useIncident(id)` wraps `useAgent<IncidentState>`. It merges `delta` broadcasts into a streaming buffer keyed by `messageId` and exposes the callables. `useIncidents()` polls the list and fires `useNotifications()` when a new unread row appears (Notification API, only after permission is granted from a button click).
730	
731	*As built (WP-19):*
732	- **Polling:** `useIncidents` polls every 10 s while the tab is visible, reloads at once when the tab comes back, on `INCIDENTS_CHANGED`, and on navigation. `newlyUnread` diffs against the previous load and announces nothing on the first one.
733	- **Unread:** a dot, bold text and the word "New" on the row, plus a "n new" badge on the group label. Narration and automatic status changes (monitor ticks, watch checks) set unread; the user's own changes don't.
734	- **Read:** `IncidentView` (owner) calls the `markRead` callable when it opens, and on new replies or status while the tab is visible.
735	- **Browser notifications:** one per newly unread row (`tag` = incident ID), except the incident open in a visible tab. Clicking one focuses the tab and opens the incident.
736	- **`NotificationSettings`** (the TDD's `WebhookSettings`, in the sidebar): browser on / turn on / blocked; webhook save (`SensitiveInput`), masked hint, send test, remove; "not set up on this deployment" when there's no key.
737	
738	*As built (WP-10):*
739	- **Pure helpers** in `web/lib/` (unit-tested in `test/unit/web/`):
740	  - `stream.ts`: the frame reducer (`stream_start` resets);
741	  - `feed.ts`: `buildFeed` lays out each run's question, then its checks (a pending check in the active run shows as Running), then its narration. The live buffer stands in for the narration until the stored one arrives;
742	  - `markdown.ts`: the narration subset parsed into a tree and rendered as React elements, never HTML. `[id]` becomes an `EvidenceChip` only for known IDs;
743	  - `incident.ts`: `otherHost` (§8.6, reuses `safety/target.ts` `parseTarget`) and `runNote`, which names the limit that ended the last run;
744	  - `api.ts`: API errors mapped to plain sentences.
745	- **Role:** the agent's `whoami()` callable gives the client its role, because the SDK doesn't tell a client its connection is readonly. Viewers get no composer, a "View only" badge in the sticky header and the `ReadOnlyBanner`.
746	- **Shared types:** `tsconfig.web.json` includes the few pure worker files the browser reuses (agent state types, categories, tool types, `safety/target.ts`, `lib/ids.ts`).
747	- **Kumo theming:** Kumo's brand token is pointed at `--color-cf-orange` (it's blue by default), so primary buttons and focus rings are orange. Below 768 px the Kumo `Sidebar` becomes an off-canvas sheet with a trigger.
748	- **Evidence:** the check card's evidence is a plain disclosure `<button aria-expanded>` that lazy-loads `getEvidence` into `CodeBlock`.
749	- **Vite:** `optimizeDeps.include` lists `agents/react` and `wouter`. Otherwise Vite discovered them on the first load and reloaded mid-render ("Invalid hook call").
750	
751	### 12.4 Design system (D17)
752	
753	`web/styles.css`:
754	
755	```css
756	@source "../node_modules/@cloudflare/kumo/dist/**/*.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}";
757	@import "@cloudflare/kumo/styles/tailwind";   /* must precede tailwindcss */
758	@import "tailwindcss";
759	@import "@fontsource-variable/hanken-grotesk";
760	@import "@fontsource-variable/jetbrains-mono";
761	
762	@theme {
763	  --font-sans:    "STK Bureau Sans", "Hanken Grotesk Variable", system-ui, sans-serif;
764	  --font-display: "FT Kunst Grotesk", "Hanken Grotesk Variable", system-ui, sans-serif;
765	  --font-mono:    "Apercu Mono Pro", "JetBrains Mono Variable", ui-monospace, monospace;
766	  --color-cf-orange: #ff5e1f;  --color-cf-orange-hover: #ff7038;  --color-cf-orange-muted: #bf4c14;
767	  --radius-xs: .125rem; --radius-sm: .25rem; --radius-md: .375rem; --radius-lg: .5rem; --radius-xl: .75rem;
768	}
769	:root {
770	  --cf-bg-100: light-dark(#ffffff, #151414);  --cf-bg-200: light-dark(#fdfdfc, #191817);
771	  --cf-bg-300: light-dark(#f9f7f6, #2a2927);  --cf-fg: light-dark(#262626, #f0e3de);
772	  --cf-fg-muted: light-dark(#737373, #a3a3a3); --cf-border: light-dark(#f0e3de, #f0e3de20);
773	}
774	#root { isolation: isolate; }
775	```
776	
777	- Token values come from cloudflare.com's live CSS (2026-10-14).
778	- **Never use Tailwind `dark:` variants.** Kumo uses `light-dark()`, and `theme.ts` sets `data-mode` on `<html>` from `prefers-color-scheme` and listens for changes.
779	- Headings use `font-display` at weight 500. Hostnames, evidence IDs and raw evidence use `font-mono`.
780	- Orange is only for primary CTAs (Send, Approve) and highlight rings. Everything else uses Kumo's neutral variants.
781	- **Kumo components used:** `Sidebar`, `Button`, `Badge`, `LayerCard`, `Surface`, `Collapsible`, `Code`, `Loader`, `Banner`, `Toast`, `Dialog`, `ClipboardText`, `Input`, `SensitiveInput`, `Select`/`Radio`, `Table`, `Tooltip`, `Empty`. Before building, run `npx @cloudflare/kumo ls` and read `node_modules/@cloudflare/kumo/ai/USAGE.md` for exact props.
782	- **Accessibility:** every control works from the keyboard, the composer submits with Enter (Shift+Enter for a new line), Approve and Reject are real `<button>`s, the stream region has `aria-live="polite"`, and status is never shown by color alone.
783	
784	## 13. Observability

### 🤖 Claude · 02:28:30

All the UI changes are in and committed on `dev` (`f21232b`, plus `36805d0` for the board). Typecheck, lint and all 599 tests pass, 4 of them new. I also checked it in a real browser: light and dark mode, at desktop, tablet and phone widths. There was no sideways scrolling and no console errors.

**What changed, by your list:**
- **Orange button:** a new reusable `CtaButton` (orange pill) in [CtaButton.tsx](web/components/CtaButton.tsx). It's used for New incident, Send, Approve, Watch and Save webhook.
- **"x new" badge:** now a light-orange pill. An unread row gets an orange dot and a bold name; the word "New" is read out only by screen readers.
- **Slack + Discord:** both logos sit in a row and are grey. Once you save a webhook, that app's logo shows in its brand colour.
- **Notifications:** now an on/off switch in the header, left of GitHub. A website can't take back browser permission once it's given, so "off" means the app stops sending them, remembered in this browser.
- **Status colours:** all in one file, [status.ts](web/lib/status.ts):
  - Investigating is orange, Awaiting approval purple, Mitigated grey, Resolved green.
  - Checks: Running orange, Pass green, Fail red, Error yellow.
  - The watchlist and approval cards use the same colours.
- **Right bar:** Demo service, then a dashed line, then Watchlist. On smaller screens both fold into one "Demo & watchlist" drop-down.
- **Header:** orange cloud × Incident Copilot. Kumo (the UI kit) already ships the official Cloudflare logo, so I used that instead of adding my own SVG file.
- **Background:** a faint dot grid behind the middle area only, plus a soft warm glow on the New incident page.

**Things I decided along the way:**
- **Approval card border:** a pending approval card had an orange border; I made it purple to match "waiting on you".
- **Switch colour:** Kumo builds its switch in blue and ignores our orange setting, so I added a small style rule in `styles.css` to make it orange. It's now orange in both modes.

TDD §12.1–12.4 now describe the new layout, colours and switch. TASKS.md has the WP-27 row, a decisions-log entry and the UI map row, and PROMPTS.md has this conversation.

I left your dev server on port 5173 alone and ran mine on 5190, which I've stopped. The screenshots are in my scratchpad folder if you want to look. Nothing is pushed; the dev → main PR is yours to raise when you're ready.
