## Approved Plan:
# WP-27b UI polish follow-ups

## Context
WP-27 (`f21232b`) shipped the new look. After reviewing it, the user asked for final touches: centred CTA text,
spacing, exact column alignment, matching headings on both sidebars, a tooltip on "Watch my demo", a dashed empty
state, status colours for the demo mode and the diagnosis, and a personal footer. Decisions below came from the grilling session.

## Decisions (agreed)
| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| Q1 | Coloured pills for **Demo "Now:"** (Healthy green, a problem red, Slow yellow) and the **diagnosis next to the incident status** (Healthy green, Inconclusive grey, any real problem red) |
| Q2 | One heading style on both sides: **UPPERCASE, 13px**, semibold, muted, letter-spaced (INCIDENTS, NOTIFICATIONS, DEMO SERVICE, WATCHLIST) |
| Q3 | Footer: **"Built on Cloudflare, with ♥ from Sahil"**, orange heart icon, "Sahil" links to github.com/sahilsaleem2907 |
| Q4 | "Watch my demo" tooltip: **"Add your demo site to the watchlist."** |

## Changes
1. **Centre "New incident"** — `web/components/CtaButton.tsx`: add `justify-center` to both wrappers' base classes (every CTA centres its label, not just this one).
2. **Webhook form gap** — `web/components/WebhookSettings.tsx`: form `gap-1.5` → `gap-3` between the URL field and Save.
3. **Alignment**
   - Left sidebar: Kumo's group label text uses `px-3`; `WebhookSettings` content uses `px-2` → change to `px-3` so it lines up under NOTIFICATIONS (incident rows and "No incidents yet." already sit at `px-3`).
   - Right column: "Watch my demo" (ghost button) has its own inner padding, so its text sits a few px right of the section edge → remove the offset (`-ml-2` or `px-0`) so it lines up with the WATCHLIST label and input.
   - Check again in screenshots; fix any other edge that's off.
4. **Headings** — `web/styles.css` `.section-label`: 12px → 13px. Left: pass the same class through `Sidebar.GroupLabel`'s children (`<span className="section-label">Incidents</span>`, same for Notifications) in `IncidentSidebar.tsx` and `WebhookSettings.tsx`. Kumo's inner `text-sm font-medium text-kumo-subtle` is overridden by the inner span's class.
5. **Tooltip** — `WatchlistPanel.tsx`: wrap the "Watch my demo" button in Kumo `Tooltip content="Add your demo site to the watchlist."` (already used by `NotificationToggle`).
6. **Dashed empty state** — `WatchlistPanel.tsx`: "Nothing watched yet…" in a box with a dashed border (`border border-dashed border-(--grid-dash)`, `rounded-lg`, padding), text centred.
7. **Status colours**
   - `web/lib/status.ts`: add `DEMO_MODE_TONE: Record<DemoMode, Tone>` (healthy green; errors, redirect_loop, bad_cache red; slow yellow) and `categoryTone(category)` (healthy green, inconclusive grey, everything else red).
   - `DemoPanel.tsx`: "Now:" renders one `StatusPill` per mode (with icon: CheckCircle / XCircle / Timer) instead of joined text.
   - `IncidentView.tsx`: "· Origin 5xx" becomes `<StatusPill tone={categoryTone(c)}>` with `CATEGORY_TEXT` (icon + words, TDD §12.4).
8. **Footer** — `AppShell.tsx`: "Built on Cloudflare, with <HeartIcon weight=fill, orange, aria-hidden/> from <a href=github.com/sahilsaleem2907>Sahil</a>" (screen readers: "with love from Sahil").
9. **Docs / tracking**: TDD §12.1 (footer, headings), §12.2 colour table (+ Demo and Diagnosis columns), TASKS.md WP-27b row + log line, PROMPTS.md entry. Commit on `dev`.

## Tests
- `test/unit/web/status.test.ts`: pin `DEMO_MODE_TONE` and `categoryTone` (healthy green, inconclusive grey, origin_5xx red; every `DEMO_MODES` entry has a tone).

## Verification
1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`.
2. `pnpm dev --port 5190` (leave the user's 5173 alone) + the scratchpad Playwright script, light and dark, 1440 / 900 / 390 px:
   - "New incident" label centred; webhook URL and Save have clear space.
   - Every left/right column edge lines up with its heading (measure x of label vs content with `boundingBox()`).
   - Headings identical on both sides (computed font-size 13px, uppercase).
   - Hover "Watch my demo" → tooltip shows; empty watchlist is a dashed centred box.
   - Demo "Now:" pills change colour as modes change; incident header shows a red "Origin 5xx" / green "Healthy" pill.
   - Footer text and link.

```

</details>

### 🤖 Claude · 02:43:38

All eight changes are in and committed on `dev` (`a2f4461`, plus `e9b7a29` for the board). Typecheck, lint and all 601 tests pass, 2 of them new. I checked the result in a real browser in light and dark mode.

- **New incident:** the label is centred. This applies to every orange button.
- **Webhook form:** there's more space between the URL field and Save.
- **Alignment:** I measured it in the browser. Every block's text starts exactly where its heading does: 26px on the left, 1137px on the right. The Notifications block was 4px off and "Watch my demo" was 6px off; both are fixed.
- **Headings:** INCIDENTS, NOTIFICATIONS, DEMO SERVICE and WATCHLIST all use one shared style (uppercase, 13px). INCIDENTS now also shows when the list is empty; before, you only saw "No incidents yet."
- **Tooltip:** "Watch my demo" now shows "Add your demo site to the watchlist." on hover or keyboard focus.
- **Empty watchlist:** the text sits in a dashed box, centred.
- **Status colours:**
  - Demo "Now:" is a pill: Healthy green, 500 errors / Redirect loop / Bad caching red, Slow yellow.
  - The diagnosis next to the incident status is a pill too: "Origin 5xx" red, "Healthy" green, "Inconclusive" grey.
- **Footer:** "Built on Cloudflare, with ♥ from Sahil". The heart is orange and "Sahil" links to your GitHub.

**Bug fixed from the last round:** Kumo's tooltip wraps whatever it's given in its own button. That made the greyed-out notifications switch (shown when the browser blocks notifications) a button inside a button, which is invalid HTML. Both tooltips now avoid that, and the browser check found no nested buttons. I noted the rule in the TDD so it doesn't come back.

TDD §12.1–12.2, TASKS.md and PROMPTS.md are updated. Your server on port 5173 wasn't touched, and nothing is pushed.

### 🤖 Claude · 03:04:20

The workflows themselves are still switched on. What's off is GitHub Actions for the whole repo (repo setting `enabled: false`), so nothing can start a run, not even by hand. That gives you two ways to deploy:

**1. Deploy from your machine (no GitHub needed).** These are the same steps `deploy.yml` runs, in the same order:

```bash
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm test                                                     # what the CI job checked
npx wrangler d1 migrations apply DB --remote                  # database changes (none new since WP-19)
pnpm build                                                    # builds both Workers into dist/
npx wrangler deploy -c dist/incident_copilot_demo/wrangler.json   # demo Worker first: the main one depends on it
npx wrangler deploy                                           # main Worker; prints the live URL
node scripts/smoke.ts <that URL>                              # quick health check, costs no AI usage
```

You need to be logged in to Wrangler (`npx wrangler whoami`; `npx wrangler login` if not).

**2. Turn Actions back on and start the deploy by hand.**

```bash
gh api -X PUT repos/sahilsaleem2907/cf_ai_incident_copilot/actions/permissions -F enabled=true
gh workflow run deploy.yml --ref main
gh run watch
```

One catch: today's work (WP-27, WP-27b, the icon) is on `dev` but not yet on `main`, so deploying `main` won't include it. `--ref dev` would deploy it, but it skips the dev → main PR and Sift review you normally do. Either way, after deploying, set `WEBHOOK_KEY` if you haven't (`openssl rand -base64 32 | npx wrangler secret put WEBHOOK_KEY`); without it the Slack/Discord webhooks stay off on the live site.

If you want today's changes live without GitHub, option 1 from `dev` is the direct route. I haven't run anything, since it changes the live site. Say the word and I'll run option 1 for you, stopping if the tests or the health check fail.

import { cn } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { MessageView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import type { Evidence } from "../hooks/useIncident";
import type { FeedItem } from "../lib/feed";
import { ApprovalCard } from "./ApprovalCard";
import { CheckCard } from "./CheckCard";
import { Markdown } from "./Markdown";

type Props = {
  feed: FeedItem[];
  knownIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  getEvidence: (id: string) => Promise<Evidence | null>;
  /** Owner only; viewers get cards without buttons. */
  onDecide?: (id: string, decision: "approve" | "reject") => Promise<unknown>;
};

/** The incident conversation: messages, check cards in run order, and the live narration (TDD §12.2). */
export function ChatPane({ feed, knownIds, getEvidence, onDecide }: Props) {
  const end = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const last = feed.at(-1);
  const lastKey = last ? `${last.key}:${last.kind === "stream" ? last.text.length : ""}` : "";
  useEffect(() => {
    end.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "end" });
  }, [lastKey]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-3 px-4 py-6">
      {feed.map((item) => {
        switch (item.kind) {
          case "message":
            return <MessageBubble key={item.key} message={item.message} knownIds={knownIds} />;
          case "check":
            return <CheckCard key={item.key} check={item.check} status={item.status} getEvidence={getEvidence} />;
          case "approval":
            return <ApprovalCard key={item.key} approval={item.approval} knownIds={knownIds} {...(onDecide ? { onDecide } : {})} />;
          case "stream":
            return (
              <div key={item.key} aria-live="polite" aria-busy="true" className="rounded-lg bg-(--cf-bg-200) p-4">
                <Markdown text={item.text || "…"} knownIds={knownIds} />
              </div>
            );
        }
      })}
      <div ref={end} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function MessageBubble({ message, knownIds }: { message: MessageView; knownIds: ReadonlySet<string> }) {
  if (message.role === "user") {
    return (
      <div className="ml-auto max-w-[85%] rounded-lg bg-(--cf-bg-300) px-4 py-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">
        <span className="sr-only">You: </span>
        {message.content}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "rounded-lg p-4",
        message.role === "system" ? "border border-dashed border-(--cf-border) text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)" : "bg-(--cf-bg-200)",
      )}
    >
      <Markdown text={message.content} knownIds={knownIds} />
    </div>
  );
}
import { CodeBlock, Loader, Surface } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CaretRightIcon, CertificateIcon, GlobeIcon, ListMagnifyingGlassIcon, PulseIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { CheckView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import type { ToolName } from "../../worker/tools/types";
import type { Evidence } from "../hooks/useIncident";
import { StatusBadge } from "./StatusBadge";

const ICONS: Record<ToolName, typeof GlobeIcon> = {
  dns_lookup: ListMagnifyingGlassIcon,
  http_probe: GlobeIcon,
  cert_info: CertificateIcon,
  provider_status: PulseIcon,
};

type Props = { check: CheckView; status: CheckView["status"]; getEvidence: (id: string) => Promise<Evidence | null> };

/** One check: tool, evidence ID, status and summary; the raw evidence loads on first expand (TDD §12.2). */
export function CheckCard({ check, status, getEvidence }: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [evidence, setEvidence] = useState<Evidence | null | "loading" | "error">(null);
  const Icon = ICONS[check.tool];
  const finished = status !== "pending" && status !== "running";

  const toggle = async () => {
    const next = !open;
    setOpen(next);
    if (next && (evidence === null || evidence === "error")) {
      setEvidence("loading");
      try {
        setEvidence((await getEvidence(check.evidenceId)) ?? "error");
      } catch {
        setEvidence("error");
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <Surface id={`check-${check.evidenceId}`} tabIndex={-1} className="rounded-lg border border-(--cf-border) outline-none">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3">
        <Icon aria-hidden size={18} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-(--cf-fg-muted)" />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-medium">{check.label}</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{check.evidenceId}</span>
            <StatusBadge status={status} />
          </div>
          {check.summary && <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs break-words text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{check.summary}</p>}
        </div>
        {finished && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void toggle()}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls={`evidence-${check.evidenceId}`}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
          >
            <CaretRightIcon aria-hidden size={12} className={open ? "rotate-90 transition-transform" : "transition-transform"} />
            Evidence
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {open && (
        <div id={`evidence-${check.evidenceId}`} className="border-t border-(--cf-border) p-3">
          {evidence === "loading" && <Loader size={16} />}
          {evidence === "error" && <p className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Couldn't load this evidence.</p>}
          {evidence && typeof evidence === "object" && <CodeBlock lang="jsonc" code={JSON.stringify(evidence.raw, null, 2)} />}
        </div>
      )}
    </Surface>
  );
}

  "content": "# WP-27c: scroll bug, evidence overflow, dashed loader\n\n## Context\nUser found two bugs after WP-27/27b and asked for one loader style:\n1. **Page scrolls past the footer once the chat streams.** `ChatPane` auto-scrolls with\n   `end.scrollIntoView({ block: \"end\" })` (`web/components/ChatPane.tsx`), which scrolls *every* scrollable ancestor,\n   including Kumo's `overflow-hidden` sidebar wrapper and the document, so the whole shell shifts and the footer moves away.\n2. **Evidence JSON spills outside its check card.** `CheckCard` renders Kumo `CodeBlock` (`min-w-0 … [&>pre]:p-2.5`)\n   with nothing stopping long lines, so they push past the card.\n3. **Loaders:** replace Kumo's spinner everywhere with one simple dashed loader. Kumo's `Loader` animates with SVG\n   `<animate>` (can't be restyled by CSS), and Kumo `Button`'s `loading` prop renders that same `Loader` internally.\n\n## Decisions (agreed)\n| # | Decision |\n|---|---|\n| Q1 | **Dashed ring, spinning**: a small circle of ~8 short dashes rotating slowly; same size as today's spinner |\n| Q2 | Chat shows **ring + what it's doing** while a run is active: \"Running checks…\" until checks finish, then \"Writing the diagnosis…\" until text streams; the empty answer bubble shows the ring instead of \"…\" |\n| Q3 | Evidence: **lines wrap inside the card, box capped at ~20 lines** (`max-h-80`) with its own scroll |\n| Q4 | Auto-scroll **follows only when you're at the bottom**; scrolling up stops it; sending a message always jumps to the bottom |\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Scroll — `web/components/ChatPane.tsx`, `web/styles.css`, `web/components/AppShell.tsx`\n- Replace `scrollIntoView` with scrolling the chat's own scroll container only: `const scroller = root.closest(\"main\")`;\n  `scroller.scrollTo({ top: scroller.scrollHeight })`.\n- Track \"pinned to bottom\" with a passive `scroll` listener on that container (`scrollHeight - scrollTop - clientHeight < 80`).\n  On feed change: scroll if pinned, or if the last item is a user message (just sent).\n- Safety net so nothing can scroll the page itself: `html, body { height: 100%; overflow: hidden; }` in `@layer base`,\n  and `overflow-hidden` on the AppShell root `div` (h-dvh). Only `<main>`, the sidebars and the aside scroll.\n\n### 2. Evidence — `web/components/CheckCard.tsx`\n- Card `Surface` gets `min-w-0`; evidence wrapper gets `min-w-0`.\n- `CodeBlock` className: `max-h-80 overflow-auto [&_pre]:whitespace-pre-wrap [&_pre]:break-words` (wrap, cap height, scroll inside).\n- Also `min-w-0` on `ChatPane` children wrapper if needed so flex items can shrink (check in browser).\n\n### 3. Dashed loader — new `web/components/DashedLoader.tsx`\n- Inline SVG: 8 short round-capped dashes on a circle (`stroke=\"currentColor\"`), rotated by a CSS `@keyframes spin` (~1.2 s, linear).\n  Props `size` (default 16) and `label` (default \"Loading\", `role=\"status\"`). `prefers-reduced-motion`: no rotation.\n- Replace every Kumo `Loader`:\n  - `App.tsx` \"Starting…\", `IncidentView.tsx` \"Connecting…\", `CheckCard.tsx` evidence loading,\n    `StatusBadge.tsx` Running pill, `ApprovalCard.tsx` working pill.\n- Buttons: Kumo's `loading` prop draws its own spinner. `CtaButton`/`CtaLinkButton` intercept `loading`: pass\n  `loading={false}`, `disabled`, `aria-busy`, and `icon={<DashedLoader size={14} />}`. The two plain Kumo buttons with\n  `loading` (Reject in `ApprovalCard`, Resolve in `IncidentView`) get the same treatment through a small shared\n  `loadingProps(loading, icon)` helper exported from `CtaButton.tsx` (one place, no new component per button).\n\n### 4. Chat working row — `web/lib/feed.ts`, `ChatPane.tsx`, `IncidentView.tsx`\n- Pure helper `workingLabel(feed, active: boolean): string | null`:\n  - not active → null;\n  - last item is a stream with text → null (the text is the progress);\n  - any check is `pending`/`running`, or the last item is the user's message → \"Running checks…\";\n  - otherwise → \"Writing the diagnosis…\".\n- `IncidentView` passes `working={!!state.activeRunId}` to `ChatPane`; `ChatPane` renders\n  `<DashedLoader /> {label}` (muted, `aria-live=\"polite\"`) at the end of the feed, and the empty stream bubble shows the\n  ring instead of \"…\".\n\n### 5. Docs / tracking\n- TDD §12.2 (loader, chat working row, evidence box), §12.4 (one loader; never Kumo `Loader` / Button `loading` spinner directly).\n- TASKS.md: WP-27c row (start/done, commit) + decisions-log line (scrollIntoView cause). PROMPTS.md entry. Commit on `dev`.\n\n## Tests\n- `test/unit/web/stream-feed.test.ts` (existing feed tests): `workingLabel` cases — inactive, just sent, check running,\n  checks done no text, streaming with text.\n\n## Verification\n1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`.\n2. `pnpm dev --port 5190` (leave the user's 5173 alone; `LLM_MODE=mock`) + scratchpad Playwright:\n   - Send a message: \"Running checks…\" with the ring appears, then \"Writing the diagnosis…\", then the answer.\n   - During and after streaming: `document.scrollingElement.scrollTop === 0` and the footer's bounding box stays at the\n     bottom of the viewport; only `<main>` has scrolled.\n   - Scroll up mid-stream → stays put; scroll back to bottom → follows again.\n   - Open Evidence on an HTTP probe: the code box's right edge ≤ the card's right edge, height ≤ 320 px, lines wrap.\n   - Buttons in loading state show the dashed ring (Send, Approve), Running pill shows it; light + dark; 390 px wide.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# WP-27c: scroll bug, evidence overflow, dashed loader

## Context
User found two bugs after WP-27/27b and asked for one loader style:
1. **Page scrolls past the footer once the chat streams.** `ChatPane` auto-scrolls with
   `end.scrollIntoView({ block: "end" })` (`web/components/ChatPane.tsx`), which scrolls *every* scrollable ancestor,
   including Kumo's `overflow-hidden` sidebar wrapper and the document, so the whole shell shifts and the footer moves away.
2. **Evidence JSON spills outside its check card.** `CheckCard` renders Kumo `CodeBlock` (`min-w-0 … [&>pre]:p-2.5`)
   with nothing stopping long lines, so they push past the card.
3. **Loaders:** replace Kumo's spinner everywhere with one simple dashed loader. Kumo's `Loader` animates with SVG
   `<animate>` (can't be restyled by CSS), and Kumo `Button`'s `loading` prop renders that same `Loader` internally.

## Decisions (agreed)
| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| Q1 | **Dashed ring, spinning**: a small circle of ~8 short dashes rotating slowly; same size as today's spinner |
| Q2 | Chat shows **ring + what it's doing** while a run is active: "Running checks…" until checks finish, then "Writing the diagnosis…" until text streams; the empty answer bubble shows the ring instead of "…" |
| Q3 | Evidence: **lines wrap inside the card, box capped at ~20 lines** (`max-h-80`) with its own scroll |
| Q4 | Auto-scroll **follows only when you're at the bottom**; scrolling up stops it; sending a message always jumps to the bottom |

## Changes

### 1. Scroll — `web/components/ChatPane.tsx`, `web/styles.css`, `web/components/AppShell.tsx`
- Replace `scrollIntoView` with scrolling the chat's own scroll container only: `const scroller = root.closest("main")`;
  `scroller.scrollTo({ top: scroller.scrollHeight })`.
- Track "pinned to bottom" with a passive `scroll` listener on that container (`scrollHeight - scrollTop - clientHeight < 80`).
  On feed change: scroll if pinned, or if the last item is a user message (just sent).
- Safety net so nothing can scroll the page itself: `html, body { height: 100%; overflow: hidden; }` in `@layer base`,
  and `overflow-hidden` on the AppShell root `div` (h-dvh). Only `<main>`, the sidebars and the aside scroll.

### 2. Evidence — `web/components/CheckCard.tsx`
- Card `Surface` gets `min-w-0`; evidence wrapper gets `min-w-0`.
- `CodeBlock` className: `max-h-80 overflow-auto [&_pre]:whitespace-pre-wrap [&_pre]:break-words` (wrap, cap height, scroll inside).
- Also `min-w-0` on `ChatPane` children wrapper if needed so flex items can shrink (check in browser).

### 3. Dashed loader — new `web/components/DashedLoader.tsx`
- Inline SVG: 8 short round-capped dashes on a circle (`stroke="currentColor"`), rotated by a CSS `@keyframes spin` (~1.2 s, linear).
  Props `size` (default 16) and `label` (default "Loading", `role="status"`). `prefers-reduced-motion`: no rotation.
- Replace every Kumo `Loader`:
  - `App.tsx` "Starting…", `IncidentView.tsx` "Connecting…", `CheckCard.tsx` evidence loading,
    `StatusBadge.tsx` Running pill, `ApprovalCard.tsx` working pill.
- Buttons: Kumo's `loading` prop draws its own spinner. `CtaButton`/`CtaLinkButton` intercept `loading`: pass
  `loading={false}`, `disabled`, `aria-busy`, and `icon={<DashedLoader size={14} />}`. The two plain Kumo buttons with
  `loading` (Reject in `ApprovalCard`, Resolve in `IncidentView`) get the same treatment through a small shared
  `loadingProps(loading, icon)` helper exported from `CtaButton.tsx` (one place, no new component per button).

### 4. Chat working row — `web/lib/feed.ts`, `ChatPane.tsx`, `IncidentView.tsx`
- Pure helper `workingLabel(feed, active: boolean): string | null`:
  - not active → null;
  - last item is a stream with text → null (the text is the progress);
  - any check is `pending`/`running`, or the last item is the user's message → "Running checks…";
  - otherwise → "Writing the diagnosis…".
- `IncidentView` passes `working={!!state.activeRunId}` to `ChatPane`; `ChatPane` renders
  `<DashedLoader /> {label}` (muted, `aria-live="polite"`) at the end of the feed, and the empty stream bubble shows the
  ring instead of "…".

### 5. Docs / tracking
- TDD §12.2 (loader, chat working row, evidence box), §12.4 (one loader; never Kumo `Loader` / Button `loading` spinner directly).
- TASKS.md: WP-27c row (start/done, commit) + decisions-log line (scrollIntoView cause). PROMPTS.md entry. Commit on `dev`.

## Tests
- `test/unit/web/stream-feed.test.ts` (existing feed tests): `workingLabel` cases — inactive, just sent, check running,
  checks done no text, streaming with text.

## Verification
1. `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test`.
2. `pnpm dev --port 5190` (leave the user's 5173 alone; `LLM_MODE=mock`) + scratchpad Playwright:
   - Send a message: "Running checks…" with the ring appears, then "Writing the diagnosis…", then the answer.
   - During and after streaming: `document.scrollingElement.scrollTop === 0` and the footer's bounding box stays at the
     bottom of the viewport; only `<main>` has scrolled.
   - Scroll up mid-stream → stays put; scroll back to bottom → follows again.
   - Open Evidence on an HTTP probe: the code box's right edge ≤ the card's right edge, height ≤ 320 px, lines wrap.
   - Buttons in loading state show the dashed ring (Send, Approve), Running pill shows it; light + dark; 390 px wide.

Still when the user prefers reduced motion.\n */\nexport function DashedLoader({ size = 16, label = \"Loading\", className = \"\" }: { size?: number; label?: string; className?: string }) {\n  return (\n    <svg\n      role=\"status\"\n      aria-label={label}\n      width={size}\n      height={size}\n      viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n      fill=\"none\"\n      stroke=\"currentColor\"\n      strokeWidth={2.25}\n      strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n      className={`shrink-0 animate-spin [animation-duration:1.4s] motion-reduce:animate-none ${className}`}\n    >\n      {/* Circumference of r=9 is 56.5: 8 dashes of 3.6 with gaps of 3.47. */}\n      <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"9\" strokeDasharray=\"3.6 3.47\" />\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { Button, cn, LinkButton, type ButtonProps, type LinkButtonProps } from \"@cloudflare/kumo\";\nimport { DashedLoader } from \"./DashedLoader\";\n\n/**\n * Kumo's `loading` prop draws its own spinner. Spread this instead so a busy button shows our `DashedLoader` in\n * place of its icon and can't be clicked again: `<Button {...loadingProps(busy, Icon, disabled)}>`.\n */\nexport function loadingProps(loading: boolean | undefined, icon: ButtonProps[\"icon\"], disabled?: boolean) {\n  return loading\n    ? { loading: false, disabled: true, \"aria-busy\": true, icon: <DashedLoader size={14} label=\"Working\" /> }\n    : { loading: false, disabled, icon };\n}\n\n/**\n * The app's main action: Kumo's primary (Cloudflare orange) button as a pill (TDD §12.2, §12.4).\n * Same props as Kumo's `Button`; `variant` is fixed; `loading` shows the dashed loader.\n */\nexport function CtaButton({ className, loading, icon, disabled, ...props }: ButtonProps) {\n  return <Button {...props} {...loadingProps(loading, icon, disabled)} variant=\"primary\" className={cn(\"justify-center rounded-full\", className)} />;\n}\n\n/** `CtaButton` that navigates (an anchor), for links such as \"New incident\". */\nexport function CtaLinkButton({ className, ...props }: LinkButtonProps) {\n  return <LinkButton {...props} variant=\"primary\" className={cn(\"justify-center rounded-full\", className)} />;\n}\n"
}

describe("buildFeed", () => {
  it("puts each run's checks under its question and before its narration", () => {
    const feed = buildFeed(
      state({
        messages: [msg("u1", "run-1", "user", 1), msg("narr-run-1", "run-1", "assistant", 5), msg("u2", "run-2", "user", 6), msg("narr-run-2", "run-2", "assistant", 9)],
        checks: [check("dns-1", "run-1"), check("http-1", "run-1"), check("http-2", "run-2")],
      }),
      {},
    );
    expect(keys(feed)).toEqual(["msg-u1", "check-dns-1", "check-http-1", "msg-narr-run-1", "msg-u2", "check-http-2", "msg-narr-run-2"]);
  });

  it("shows pending checks of the active run as running, and the live narration after the checks", () => {
    const feed = buildFeed(state({ messages: [msg("u1", "run-1", "user", 1)], checks: [check("http-1", "run-1", "pending")], activeRunId: "run-1" }), {
      "narr-run-1": { messageId: "narr-run-1", text: "**Likely", done: false },
    });
    expect(feed.map((i) => i.kind)).toEqual(["message", "check", "stream"]);
    expect(feed[1]).toMatchObject({ kind: "check", status: "running" });
    expect(feed[2]).toMatchObject({ kind: "stream", text: "**Likely" });
  });

  it("replaces the stream with the stored narration", () => {
    const feed = buildFeed(state({ messages: [msg("u1", "run-1", "user", 1), msg("narr-run-1", "run-1", "assistant", 2)] }), {
      "narr-run-1": { messageId: "narr-run-1", text: "partial", done: true },
    });
    expect(keys(feed)).toEqual(["msg-u1", "msg-narr-run-1"]);
  });

  it("keeps a pending check of a finished run as pending, and shows orphan checks at the end", () => {
    const feed = buildFeed(state({ messages: [msg("sys", null, "system", 0)], checks: [check("http-1", "run-9", "pending")] }), {});
    expect(feed).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ key: "msg-sys" }), expect.objectContaining({ key: "check-http-1", status: "pending" })]);
  });
});

### 🤖 Claude · 03:29:25

The evidence bug and the loader are fixed and checked in the browser. The scroll bug is fixed at its likely cause, but I couldn't reproduce it here, so please try it on your side. Everything is committed on `dev` (`0a225a0`, plus `ed4e7be` for the board) but not pushed. Typecheck, lint and all 604 tests pass (3 new).

**1. Page scrolling past the footer.**
- **Cause:** the chat kept itself at the bottom with `scrollIntoView`. That scrolls the chat column and also every container above it, up to the page itself.
- **Fix:**
  - The chat now scrolls only its own column, and only while you're at the bottom (your choice). Scroll up to read and it stays put; sending a message jumps back down.
  - The page itself is locked so it can't scroll at all, and wheel scrolling stops at the end of the chat instead of carrying on into the page.
- **Not confirmed:** I couldn't make it happen in my test browser at desktop, tablet or phone width, even with real mouse-wheel scrolling. So this removes the likely cause; I didn't watch it fix the symptom. If it still happens in your browser, tell me your window size and zoom level.

**2. Evidence JSON leaving its card.** Long lines now wrap, and the box stops at about 20 lines (320px) with its own scroll. I added a 400-character line on a phone-width screen and it still stayed inside the card.

**3. Dashed loader everywhere.** A small ring of dashes that turns slowly now replaces every spinner:
- "Starting…" and "Connecting…";
- the Running check pill and the approval's "working" pill;
- loading evidence;
- busy buttons: Send, Approve, Reject, Resolve, Watch, Save webhook.

While the copilot works, the chat now shows the ring with "Running checks…", then "Writing the diagnosis…", and it disappears once the answer starts appearing. With the mock AI each step lasts well under a second, so you'll mainly notice it with the real model.

TDD §12.2–12.4 now record the rules: one loader, and the page never scrolls. TASKS.md and PROMPTS.md are updated.
